closed system:
- reactants and products are not lost to surroundings
- energy transferred in and out of surroundings

open system
- reactants and products can be transferred in and out of
surroundings as well as energy.

dyanmic equilibrium:
- can only occur in closed systems, as if products and
reactants are constantly transferred in and out,
it would constantly shift equilibrium and dynamic 
equilibrium cannot occur ^Wyv5lxnr

1. a closed system is a chemical system where
onyl energy is allowed to be exchanged
between the surroundings and the system but
mass is not allowed to be exchanged between the
surroundings and system.

open system is chemical system where both energy
and matter can be exchanged between the surroudnings
and system.

dynamic equilibrium is a state achieved by a system in 
whch a reversible equilibrium reaction is occuring. 
rate of forward = rate of reverse
and so there is no change in macroscopic proeprties
of the system
we refer to the equlibrium as dyanmic because product
molecules are still forming reaectant particles via
reverse reaction
and reactants are still forming product molecules.
however, rate is constnat and equal ^0bFyV2H3

by adding sodium hypchloride, [ClO-(aq)] increases.

system establishes equilibrium by counteracting the increase in 
[ClO-], thus system favours the forward reaction, as that consumes
and thus decreases [ClO-].

thus equilibrium shifts to the right and thus [HClO(aq)] increases
thus yield of HClO (aq) increases. ^RWMVtLO0

Closed system is a system that only allows movement
of energy between system and surroundings but not mass.
Open system allows both movement of energy and mass between
system and surroundings.
Dynamic equilibrium is the process that only occurs if rate of reverse
and forward reaction are equal  ^Z1rHbqSk

and so there is no change in macroscopic properties of the systme
we refer to equilibrium as dynamic because product molecules are still forming
reactant particles via reverse rxn and reactants are still forming
product molecules - hwoever rate happenign is constant and equal ^veOeiq7M

		{
			"id": "Wyv5lxnr",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -227.8053088568444,
			"y": 778.4437850774652,
			"width": 595.5795288085938,
			"height": 325,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#1e1e1e",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 88404296,
			"version": 512,
			"versionNonce": 591649848,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "closed system:\n- reactants and products are not lost to surroundings\n- energy transferred in and out of surroundings\n\nopen system\n- reactants and products can be transferred in and out of\nsurroundings as well as energy.\n\ndyanmic equilibrium:\n- can only occur in closed systems, as if products and\nreactants are constantly transferred in and out,\nit would constantly shift equilibrium and dynamic \nequilibrium cannot occur",
			"rawText": "closed system:\n- reactants and products are not lost to surroundings\n- energy transferred in and out of surroundings\n\nopen system\n- reactants and products can be transferred in and out of\nsurroundings as well as energy.\n\ndyanmic equilibrium:\n- can only occur in closed systems, as if products and\nreactants are constantly transferred in and out,\nit would constantly shift equilibrium and dynamic \nequilibrium cannot occur",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 318,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "closed system:\n- reactants and products are not lost to surroundings\n- energy transferred in and out of surroundings\n\nopen system\n- reactants and products can be transferred in and out of\nsurroundings as well as energy.\n\ndyanmic equilibrium:\n- can only occur in closed systems, as if products and\nreactants are constantly transferred in and out,\nit would constantly shift equilibrium and dynamic \nequilibrium cannot occur",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
		{
			"id": "0bFyV2H3",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -176.31572552351116,
			"y": 1232.902118410799,
			"width": 548.2395629882812,
			"height": 500,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#e03131",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 228702264,
			"version": 867,
			"versionNonce": 144778056,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "1. a closed system is a chemical system where\nonyl energy is allowed to be exchanged\nbetween the surroundings and the system but\nmass is not allowed to be exchanged between the\nsurroundings and system.\n\nopen system is chemical system where both energy\nand matter can be exchanged between the surroudnings\nand system.\n\ndynamic equilibrium is a state achieved by a system in \nwhch a reversible equilibrium reaction is occuring. \nrate of forward = rate of reverse\nand so there is no change in macroscopic proeprties\nof the system\nwe refer to the equlibrium as dyanmic because product\nmolecules are still forming reaectant particles via\nreverse reaction\nand reactants are still forming product molecules.\nhowever, rate is constnat and equal",
			"rawText": "1. a closed system is a chemical system where\nonyl energy is allowed to be exchanged\nbetween the surroundings and the system but\nmass is not allowed to be exchanged between the\nsurroundings and system.\n\nopen system is chemical system where both energy\nand matter can be exchanged between the surroudnings\nand system.\n\ndynamic equilibrium is a state achieved by a system in \nwhch a reversible equilibrium reaction is occuring. \nrate of forward = rate of reverse\nand so there is no change in macroscopic proeprties\nof the system\nwe refer to the equlibrium as dyanmic because product\nmolecules are still forming reaectant particles via\nreverse reaction\nand reactants are still forming product molecules.\nhowever, rate is constnat and equal",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 493,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "1. a closed system is a chemical system where\nonyl energy is allowed to be exchanged\nbetween the surroundings and the system but\nmass is not allowed to be exchanged between the\nsurroundings and system.\n\nopen system is chemical system where both energy\nand matter can be exchanged between the surroudnings\nand system.\n\ndynamic equilibrium is a state achieved by a system in \nwhch a reversible equilibrium reaction is occuring. \nrate of forward = rate of reverse\nand so there is no change in macroscopic proeprties\nof the system\nwe refer to the equlibrium as dyanmic because product\nmolecules are still forming reaectant particles via\nreverse reaction\nand reactants are still forming product molecules.\nhowever, rate is constnat and equal",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
		{
			"id": "RWMVtLO0",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -393.9823921901787,
			"y": 1881.7198267441304,
			"width": 688.9993896484375,
			"height": 200,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#1e1e1e",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 802553400,
			"version": 481,
			"versionNonce": 669750584,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "by adding sodium hypchloride, [ClO-(aq)] increases.\n\nsystem establishes equilibrium by counteracting the increase in \n[ClO-], thus system favours the forward reaction, as that consumes\nand thus decreases [ClO-].\n\nthus equilibrium shifts to the right and thus [HClO(aq)] increases\nthus yield of HClO (aq) increases.",
			"rawText": "by adding sodium hypchloride, [ClO-(aq)] increases.\n\nsystem establishes equilibrium by counteracting the increase in \n[ClO-], thus system favours the forward reaction, as that consumes\nand thus decreases [ClO-].\n\nthus equilibrium shifts to the right and thus [HClO(aq)] increases\nthus yield of HClO (aq) increases.",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 193,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "by adding sodium hypchloride, [ClO-(aq)] increases.\n\nsystem establishes equilibrium by counteracting the increase in \n[ClO-], thus system favours the forward reaction, as that consumes\nand thus decreases [ClO-].\n\nthus equilibrium shifts to the right and thus [HClO(aq)] increases\nthus yield of HClO (aq) increases.",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},

		{
			"id": "Z1rHbqSk",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -394.8938505235131,
			"y": 2383.891701744128,
			"width": 692.0194091796875,
			"height": 150,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#1e1e1e",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 2078912056,
			"version": 412,
			"versionNonce": 1476503352,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "Closed system is a system that only allows movement\nof energy between system and surroundings but not mass.\nOpen system allows both movement of energy and mass between\nsystem and surroundings.\nDynamic equilibrium is the process that only occurs if rate of reverse\nand forward reaction are equal ",
			"rawText": "Closed system is a system that only allows movement\nof energy between system and surroundings but not mass.\nOpen system allows both movement of energy and mass between\nsystem and surroundings.\nDynamic equilibrium is the process that only occurs if rate of reverse\nand forward reaction are equal ",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 143,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "Closed system is a system that only allows movement\nof energy between system and surroundings but not mass.\nOpen system allows both movement of energy and mass between\nsystem and surroundings.\nDynamic equilibrium is the process that only occurs if rate of reverse\nand forward reaction are equal ",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
		{
			"id": "veOeiq7M",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -319.2011421901798,
			"y": 2569.4125350774616,
			"width": 768.8193359375,
			"height": 100,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#e03131",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,
			"roundness": null,
			"seed": 1786242376,
			"version": 340,
			"versionNonce": 1605994568,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "and so there is no change in macroscopic properties of the systme\nwe refer to equilibrium as dynamic because product molecules are still forming\nreactant particles via reverse rxn and reactants are still forming\nproduct molecules - hwoever rate happenign is constant and equal",
			"rawText": "and so there is no change in macroscopic properties of the systme\nwe refer to equilibrium as dynamic because product molecules are still forming\nreactant particles via reverse rxn and reactants are still forming\nproduct molecules - hwoever rate happenign is constant and equal",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 93,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "and so there is no change in macroscopic properties of the systme\nwe refer to equilibrium as dynamic because product molecules are still forming\nreactant particles via reverse rxn and reactants are still forming\nproduct molecules - hwoever rate happenign is constant and equal",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
		{
			"id": "LRmDY8Bb",
			"type": "text",
			"x": -177.5240588568469,
			"y": 3113.886493410796,
			"width": 668.679443359375,
			"height": 275,
			"angle": 0,
			"strokeColor": "#1e1e1e",
			"backgroundColor": "transparent",
			"fillStyle": "solid",
			"strokeWidth": 1,
			"strokeStyle": "dashed",
			"roughness": 1,
			"opacity": 100,
			"groupIds": [],
			"frameId": null,

			"seed": 1987441736,
			"version": 545,
			"versionNonce": 951582776,
			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529108,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "Closed system - only allows movement of energy and not\nmovement of mass.\nOpen system - allows both movement of energy and mass.\n\nDynamic equilibrium - occurs when rates of forward and \nreverse reaction are equal. - no change n macroscopic properties\nof the system.\n\ndynamic bcos prod molecules are still forming reactant particles via\nreverse reaction and reactants are still forming product molecules\nhowever rate is constant and equal.",
			"rawText": "Closed system - only allows movement of energy and not\nmovement of mass.\nOpen system - allows both movement of energy and mass.\n\nDynamic equilibrium - occurs when rates of forward and \nreverse reaction are equal. - no change n macroscopic properties\nof the system.\n\ndynamic bcos prod molecules are still forming reactant particles via\nreverse reaction and reactants are still forming product molecules\nhowever rate is constant and equal.",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 268,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "Closed system - only allows movement of energy and not\nmovement of mass.\nOpen system - allows both movement of energy and mass.\n\nDynamic equilibrium - occurs when rates of forward and \nreverse reaction are equal. - no change n macroscopic properties\nof the system.\n\ndynamic bcos prod molecules are still forming reactant particles via\nreverse reaction and reactants are still forming product molecules\nhowever rate is constant and equal.",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},

		{

			"isDeleted": false,
			"boundElements": null,
			"updated": 1709164529109,
			"link": null,
			"locked": false,
			"text": "closed system - allows movement of energy but not mass\n\nopen system - allows movement of both energy and mass\n\ndynamic equilibrium occurs when the rates of forward \nand reverse reaction are equal, hence there are no macoscopic\nchanges of the solution.\n\ndynamic means that reactants are still forming products, and\nproducts are still forming reactants, however they are doing\nso at a constant and equal rate.",
			"rawText": "closed system - allows movement of energy but not mass\n\nopen system - allows movement of both energy and mass\n\ndynamic equilibrium occurs when the rates of forward \nand reverse reaction are equal, hence there are no macoscopic\nchanges of the solution.\n\ndynamic means that reactants are still forming products, and\nproducts are still forming reactants, however they are doing\nso at a constant and equal rate.",
			"fontSize": 20,
			"fontFamily": 1,
			"textAlign": "left",
			"verticalAlign": "top",
			"baseline": 268,
			"containerId": null,
			"originalText": "closed system - allows movement of energy but not mass\n\nopen system - allows movement of both energy and mass\n\ndynamic equilibrium occurs when the rates of forward \nand reverse reaction are equal, hence there are no macoscopic\nchanges of the solution.\n\ndynamic means that reactants are still forming products, and\nproducts are still forming reactants, however they are doing\nso at a constant and equal rate.",
			"lineHeight": 1.25
		},
